## Where we enter

First light on the 111th at Thornwater: Oryn gone north, her mule with the crew. Lira is two weeks into the season's order. A completion report sits on a desk west of the Line.

## What this movement is for

A trap priced to fit, read before it is sprung; the sixth chair filled on her own terms of service; a floor built to disarm six people that disarms five; and the first registry object the crew has ever owned, with a word in it that is a direction. Turns and promises:

- **Thornwater → Lowmarch (111–112), the first cold.** The fourth honest catalogue from the rise.
- **Pike says the Fallow line before the door is shut;** it costs a line (eleventh; "Teague's ten up"). Then what the wall cannot say: thirty years, forty-odd crews, nobody over a line and back, "Six in and six out, and the sixth a healer." **The room updates** without a word (the hide-woman, the crew of three gone grey, the carter from the first week, the boy). **Teague stands** when they come in and sits at "eleventh."
- **The second slip** on the nail of read-and-returned (protected text). Pike, unasked: "Nothing else ever… I'd not take it." Karis: "That's the carter's second circle." **Seln's anatomy** (protected lines): registry prose; the same clerk; a size, not a wage; a floor he *knows*; "pre-restriction inventory"; "The boy's the price of the case."
- **The council, the second honest collision:** Lira for walking away (the fence at forty and the walk at fifty, and pace the third circle on their own time); Brom ("Three hundred's a number a man doesn't reach for." — his father and the horse); Karis ("I would go alone."); **Seln: "Take it… People have faces. Six years I've wanted a face."** [OWNER-pending #50] Cael's sentence to the whole table (protected), then why: the assumption is wrong and cannot be known to be wrong. **Brom quotes Rooke**, the first person he has ever quoted: "Doors open both ways… I don't like it. I'm in." Lira: "I want it entered that I said walk away." The log (EXC-B7-002). Pike marks HOLD unasked and says it to the room. **Teague at the door** (protected): "If you're late back, we'll come look." / "Noted.", the first *Noted* to anyone above them on the wall.
- **Millrace (day 113):** the wheel heard before the roofs; the ford-keeper's "Five off the ridge" / "I know." **Oryn's table**: fifteen, three serious (a crushed hand, a child's chest, an old woman's knee with a ladder in it) and a dozen small with one hand each. **Ask first, refused** ("You asked. That's the rule and you kept it. The answer's no. Next."), entered first. Karis: "She was curious about you. Enter that somewhere." **Her arithmetic aloud**, finger by finger. "You're asking me to break the route." / "I'm not asking. I'm telling you where we'll be." / "That's worse. That's a healer's sentence." "I'm not going to be anybody's sixth." **Word sent up the route** by carter (late, how late, why, where to send for the child). **Terms** (protected). Lira's sixth-chair line. The fork log.
- **The reading taught on the road:** "Well?" / "May I read you?" / "Yes…" Surface at ten: Tide *there*, the first thing that has ever been there at ten. A minute of the long one: banks, one channel, a current that runs *to* things. "You're built to arrive." / "Yes… That's what I am." "He asked twice and got one." / "That's the rate."
- **The Stair from the ridge at dusk:** a bowl wider and shallower than the Ring's; a bare floor with no ring; a square hole with three lit steps and then dark. The line at forty paces ("Two hundred. I'll pace it in the morning."). **Two stakes numbered thirty-two**, one under five cold terms of scale [B6-reconciled 2026-10-11: audit A2] and one under one or two, with the **stroke and the bar**: "Newer. This one's been re-surveyed." Camp at the line.
- **Day 114, the trap (≈9,000–10,500 across two chapters).** The crossing in Seln's order. **The bolt at forty-five yards** [B6-reconciled 2026-10-11: audit A2]: "Ridge." Six hooded on the rim in road grey (two south, two east, one west, one north). **The Anchor course** closing the line behind them; Cael sees it with perception only. Bolts skip off the fused plate. **Lira runs *down***, and the unit follows. **Forty steps.** The chamber, ten strides square, its ceiling the floor; walls carved; at the middle "something cased… a cord," unread. **The doorway**: "They can shoot the bowl… They can't shoot the stair." **Oryn without hands**: lamp, eyes, two fingers; "Tape it. Leave the bolt."; "the bolt's the plug"; "I came south for exactly this. Enter that." Lira's mend reopened on the stair wall. **The stalemate, given room**: the square of grey going white and grey; a bolt sent down to stop at the thirty-first step; Brom grey and not worse; Karis with her back to the wall she would give a year to read. The design read aloud ("It thinks the floor did the same thing to all six of us."). **The order:** up alone with the one he was given. Oryn: "Ask first." / "I'm not reading anyone." / "I know. I'm saying the rule out loud so it's been said on this floor too." Lira: "Later." **The ascent, Shadow alone**, across the bowl unseen; the rim scanning for a shout that cannot exist. Once Cael is within ten feet of the knot, Anchor perception finds it; he then makes the separate purposeful unbinding, without assigning that action a measured reach, rate or price [B6-reconciled 2026-10-11: audit B1]: **the knot found and released**, the course going slack like rigging. **Over the stone, declaring**: Wind, Storm seeding the rut, Compression at contact, Ember held out. He removes one man from the plan and does not fight him. The rim breaks as professionals break. The west one looks back for a second, hood up, nothing in it. Four crossbows left; no faces. "Back."
- **Out at dark:** "There. Back… The clock started again." The bolt-head cut and the shaft drawn; an hour on Brom; a quarter on Lira ("It held a stair…"). His bill: the suite spent on a week's rest. **Seln breaks a stock across the stone**: "No faces." The log (protected). **Teague's crew at full dark** (protected exchange); he does not ask how.
- **Day 115:** Teague holds the rim unasked ("We'll hold it till you're up." / "Noted"). **C3: Karis paces the Stair's perimeter in the morning** with Lira one-armed on the line, under four rim-holders who are theirs, and gets two hundred. **Down with two lamps**: the air at the twentieth step; the chamber catalogued properly; Oryn's "What are they" / "Lines." **The kit**: open, contents in taking-out order, the cord laid toward the east wall, stake thirty-two with the stroke-and-bar beside its mallet, point bright, the floor unmarked (EXC-B7-004: "Twice, by the head… Nothing goes into this."). **The ledger read by Seln**: distances, the radius in their own hand, a designation in an index he does not know, and the last line, *third site confirmed on bearing* —. **Karis's body at "bearing."** Six hours; twenty-nine sheets; "Sheet"; Lira ahead of her every time; **forty-one** (protected); the line *between* the courses at the radius; "A measurement is a fact about a ruler…" **The kit carried up**: Brom refuses help three times. The rim-holders: the spear grounded once. The log at the stone (protected). The straightedge out on the carter's map, not yet touching anything.

## What must remain true / withheld

- **Shadow is used once in this movement**, unseen and never attributable. That is its second and last use in the book.
- **The rim saw a practitioner walk out declaring, and saw nothing work inside.** Teague saw the same and asks nothing.
- Faceless: no seals, no names, no office. The specialist is not the Ostrand-road woman: the same craft, a different weight in the laying.
- The case is untouched. The index and the stroke-and-bar are not decoded. Nobody theorizes.
- Oryn joins as a stop on her route, never as "sixth," never "join." The Stair slip's three hundred is taken and never paid.
- Teague's crew is unnamed, including in Teague's window. The healer of no Path does not appear until M8.

## What the author owns

The Lowmarch room; Millrace's texture; Oryn's patients; the road lesson's sensation; the chamber's cold; the stalemate's small human business; the ascent's suspense inside the planted rules; the rim action's choreography; the six hours; both windows.

## Where we leave pressure

Day 115, at the stone in full sun. The kit is on Brom's good shoulder and his bad one is mended once. Lira's arm is closed again with the sling higher. The ledger is in Seln's coat; twenty-nine sheets and forty-one kinds. A brass edge lies on a map. A dead man's last word was *bearing*. "We're going to go look."
